# Top-10 All Three Leaderboards: Phase 2 Sprint Plan

## Context

Phase 1 achieved leaderboard submissions for all 3 kernels but 0 are in top-10. Phase 2 incorporates intelligence from competing teams (Gemini, Infinity, OpenCode) and newly discovered aiter features.

| Kernel | Current Ranked | Top-10 Est. | Gap | Key Intel |
|--------|---------------|-------------|-----|-----------|
| **MoE** | ~152µs | ~150µs | **~2µs** | Direct CK dispatch (Infinity), 10+ untested env vars |
| **MLA** | ~72.4µs | ~50-55µs | **~20µs** | CUDA graph capture (Gemini), fast_mode=True contradiction |
| **GEMM** | ~22.7µs | ~11-12µs | **~11µs** | Split-K not activating properly, config probe needed |

**Priority**: MoE (2µs gap, closest) > MLA (20µs gap, new techniques) > GEMM (11µs gap, bottleneck shape)

**CRITICAL**: MoE production submission.py has REGRESSED from the version that achieved 152µs. Must restore first.

---

## Track 1: MoE — From ~152µs to Top-10 (~150µs)

### Task 1.0: RESTORE working MoE submission (BLOCKING)
- **Problem**: Current `submission.py` has bloated `_compute_routing_metrics()` with `torch.bincount` + Python for-loop over all experts, and complex sparsity calculations. This was NOT the version submitted to leaderboard.
- **Action**: Restore clean version based on `sprint/moe_ksplit6_257e.py` pattern:
  - Module-level `os.environ["AITER_USE_NT"] = "1"` and `os.environ["AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING"] = "1"`
  - Simple `estimated_m = topk_ids.numel() // num_experts` routing
  - Four buckets: est_m ≥80 default, ≥25 KSPLIT=2, ≥8 KSPLIT=2+BM64, <8 KSPLIT=2+BM64
  - Remove `_compute_routing_metrics()`, `_select_ksplit_and_block_m()`, sparsity calc
- **File**: `kernels/moe-mxfp4/submission.py`
- **Verify**: test → benchmark (must match ~152µs baseline)

### Task 1.1: Deep probe — discover valid env vars on runner
- **What**: Submit diagnostic that prints `dir(aiter)`, `inspect.signature(fused_moe)`, and searches fused_moe source for env var references via `inspect.getsource()`
- **Purpose**: Discover which of these 10+ env vars are actually read by fused_moe:
  `AITER_BLOCK_N`, `AITER_BLOCK_K`, `AITER_WAVES_PER_EU`, `AITER_NUM_WARPS`, `AITER_NUM_STAGES`, `AITER_OCCUPANCY`, `AITER_USE_PERSISTENT`, `AITER_STREAMK`, `AITER_MOE_PADDING`
- **File**: New probe submission (temporary)
- **Expected**: Map of env var → whether it's read, and default values

### Task 1.2: Test discovered env vars
- **What**: Based on probe results, test promising env vars one at a time
- **Candidates**: AITER_WAVES_PER_EU (occupancy tuning), AITER_STREAMK (stream-K scheduling), AITER_MOE_PADDING
- **Expected gain**: 1-3µs per effective env var

### Task 1.3: Direct CK dispatch (Infinity pattern — HIGH EFFORT)
- **What**: Replace `fused_moe()` wrapper with direct kernel calls:
  `moe_sorting_fwd()` → `fused_dynamic_mxfp4_quant_moe_sort()` → `moe_cktile2stages_gemm1()` → `silu_and_mul()` → `moe_cktile2stages_gemm2()`
- **Reference**: `opencode_infinity/moe/submission_optimized.py`
- **Risk**: Complex buffer math, CU-aware block_m selection
- **Expected gain**: 3-8µs (eliminates Python dispatch overhead in fused_moe)
- **Fallback**: Only attempt if Tasks 1.1-1.2 don't close the gap

### Safety constraint (HARD): KSPLIT=2 max
- KSPLIT≥3 → catastrophic overflow (~1e35) on both 32-expert and 257-expert shapes

### Verification
```bash

$CLI submit --no-tui --mode benchmark --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-moe-mxfp4 $KERNELS/moe-mxfp4/submission.py
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode leaderboard --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-moe-mxfp4 $KERNELS/moe-mxfp4/submission.py
```

## Track 2: MLA — From ~72.4µs to Top-10 (~50-55µs)

### Task 2.1: fast_mode A/B test
- **What**: Current production uses `fast_mode=False`. Both Gemini and Infinity teams use `fast_mode=True`. Test switching to True.
- **File**: `kernels/mixed-mla/submission.py` — `get_mla_metadata_info_v1()` and `get_mla_metadata_v1()` calls
- **Expected gain**: 2-5µs (contradicts one skill but matches two competing teams)

### Task 2.2: CUDA graph capture (Gemini pattern)
- **What**: Capture the quantize + mla_decode_fwd pipeline in a CUDA graph, replay on repeated shapes
- **Reference**: `gemini_squad/breakthroughs/mla_graph.py`
- **Key details**:
  - Use `dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8` from aiter (not manual `_quantize_fp8`)
  - `fast_mode=True` in metadata calls
  - Fixed `num_kv_splits=32`
  - Cache by `(compressed_kv.data_ptr(), k_pe.data_ptr())` pair
  - 3 warmup iterations before graph capture
- **Dead end note**: CUDA graphs regressed GEMM by +78%, but MLA is different — fewer kernel launches, no HIP kernel in the path
- **Risk**: Medium — may still regress if copy_() overhead dominates
- **Expected gain**: 5-15µs (eliminates Python dispatch for repeated shapes)

### Task 2.3: num_kv_splits optimization
- **What**: Current schedule: 1/4/8/16/32. Test more aggressive splits for bandwidth-bound shapes.
- **Expected gain**: 1-3µs

### Task 2.4: Combined best of 2.1-2.3
- **What**: Apply all improvements that benchmarked positive
- **Submit**: test → benchmark → leaderboard

### Verification
```bash
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode test --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mixed-mla $KERNELS/mixed-mla/submission.py
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode benchmark --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mixed-mla $KERNELS/mixed-mla/submission.py
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode leaderboard --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mixed-mla $KERNELS/mixed-mla/submission.py
```

## Track 3: GEMM — From ~22.7µs to Top-10 (~11-12µs)

### Task 3.1: Config diagnostic probe
- **What**: Submit probe that prints `get_GEMM_config(M, N, K)` for all 6 shapes to stderr
- **Shapes**: (4,2880,512), (16,2112,7168), (32,4096,512), (32,2880,512), (64,7168,2048), (256,3072,1536)
- **Purpose**: Determine if runner CSV returns configs vs our fallback, and what split-K values are used

### Task 3.2: Force split-K for S2 bottleneck
- **What**: S2 (M=16, N=2112, K=7168) is ~30µs — dominates geomean. Current `_get_config()` may not be applying split-K when `csv_found=True`
- **Fix**: Ensure split-K override applies regardless of CSV result for small-M large-K shapes
- **Expected gain**: 3-5µs on S2

### Task 3.3: Eliminate HIP kernel overhead for small shapes
- **What**: For M≤8, the HIP fused quant kernel has higher overhead than Triton. Current code falls through to Triton path only on HIP failure.
- **Fix**: Route M≤8 directly to Triton quant path
- **Expected gain**: 1-2µs on S1 (M=4)

### Task 3.4: Alternative tile configurations
- **What**: Based on probe results, test tile sizes beyond current fallback set
- **Current**: M≤64→32x128, M≤256→64x256, else→128x256
- **Runner has**: 32x128, 192x128 confirmed. May have 16x128, 64x128, 128x128
- **Expected gain**: 1-2µs

### Verification
```bash
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode test --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mxfp4-mm $KERNELS/mxfp4-mm/submission.py
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode benchmark --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mxfp4-mm $KERNELS/mxfp4-mm/submission.py
$CLI submit --no-tui --mode leaderboard --gpu MI355X --leaderboard amd-mxfp4-mm $KERNELS/mxfp4-mm/submission.py
```

## Execution Sequence

### Phase A: Restore + Quick Probes (3 submissions)
1. **MoE 1.0**: Restore working submission.py → test + benchmark (BLOCKING)
2. **MoE 1.1**: Submit deep env var probe → learn runner capabilities
3. **GEMM 3.1**: Submit config diagnostic probe → learn runner CSV configs

### Phase B: A/B Tests (3-4 submissions)
4. **MLA 2.1**: fast_mode=True A/B test → benchmark
5. **MoE 1.2**: Test best env var from probe results → benchmark
6. **GEMM 3.2**: Force split-K for S2 → benchmark

### Phase C: Advanced Techniques (2-4 submissions)
7. **MLA 2.2**: CUDA graph capture → test + benchmark
8. **MoE 1.3**: Direct CK dispatch (if env vars insufficient) → test + benchmark
9. **GEMM 3.3+3.4**: Small-M routing + tile optimization → benchmark

### Phase D: Final Leaderboard Submissions
10. Verify all production files with Read before submit
11. Submit best MoE → leaderboard
12. Submit best MLA → leaderboard
13. Submit best GEMM → leaderboard

---

## Known Dead Ends (DO NOT RETRY)

### Confirmed across 16+ phases:
- Custom Triton flash-decode for MLA (GEMV problem, 9-127x slower)
- Custom Triton `tl.dot_scaled` for GEMM (1.34x slower than CK ASM)
- hiprtc on runner (source scanner blocks `hipModuleLaunchKernel`, `hipModuleLoadData`)
- flash_attn / flashinfer / xformers / vllm (not installed on runner)
- CK flash_attn_varlen_func for MLA (headdim≤256 limit, MLA needs 576)
- CUDA/HIP Graphs on GEMM (+78% regression) — **but MLA may differ, test carefully**
- `doweight_stage1=True` (JIT timeout ~230s)
- KSPLIT≥3 for MoE (overflow ~1e35)
- Helion/FlyDSL (JIT crash on MI355X)
- `AITER_ONLINE_TUNE=1` (blocks for minutes)
- F.scaled_dot_product_attention for MLA (10x slower, head_dim=576)
- MLA output buffer pre-allocation (73.3µs vs 71.0µs)
- 4D matmul broadcast for MLA (9-53x regression)

### Scanner-blocked strings (amdclang++ workaround):
- `hipModuleLaunchKernel`, `hipModuleLoadData`, `hipModuleGetFunction`
- Use string concatenation: `"hip" + "Launch" + "Kernel"`

    75→def _compute_routing_metrics(topk_ids, num_experts):
    76→    """
    77→    Compute routing metrics to inform better KSPLIT decisions.
    78→    Returns expert utilization statistics for adaptive routing.
    79→    """
    80→    if topk_ids.numel() == 0:
    81→        return {"estimated_m": 0, "expert_utilization": {}, "max_expert_load": 0}
    82→
    83→    # Flatten topk_ids for easier processing
    84→    flat_ids = topk_ids.view(-1)
    85→
    86→    # Count expert usage
    87→    expert_counts = torch.bincount(flat_ids, minlength=num_experts)
    88→
    89→    # Calculate metrics
    90→    total_assignments = flat_ids.numel()
    91→    estimated_m = total_assignments // num_experts if num_experts > 0 else 0
    92→
    93→    # Expert utilization as fraction of total assignments
    94→    expert_utilization = {}
    95→    if num_experts > 0:
    96→        for expert_id in range(num_experts):
    97→            count = expert_counts[expert_id].item()
    98→            utilization = count / max(total_assignments, 1)
    99→            expert_utilization[expert_id] = utilization
   100→
   101→    max_expert_load = expert_counts.max().item() if len(expert_counts) > 0 else 0
   102→
   103→    return {
   104→        "estimated_m": estimated_m,
   105→        "expert_utilization": expert_utilization,
   106→        "max_expert_load": max_expert_load,
   107→        "total_assignments": total_assignments,
   108→        "expert_counts": expert_counts,
   109→    }
   110→

   112→def _select_ksplit_and_block_m(topk_ids, num_experts, is_large_expert):
   113→    """
   114→    Enhanced KSPLIT and BLOCK_M selection based on routing characteristics.
   115→    Uses expert utilization patterns to make more informed decisions.
   116→    """
   117→    # Compute routing metrics for better decision making
   118→    metrics = _compute_routing_metrics(topk_ids, num_experts)
   119→    estimated_m = metrics["estimated_m"]
   120→    max_expert_load = metrics["max_expert_load"]
   121→    expert_utilization = metrics["expert_utilization"]
   122→
   123→    # Calculate sparsity measure: how uneven the expert distribution is
   124→    if num_experts > 0 and len(expert_utilization) > 0:
   125→        utilizations = list(expert_utilization.values())
   126→        if utilizations:
   127→            avg_utilization = sum(utilizations) / len(utilizations)
   128→            # Sparsity: 1.0 means perfectly uniform, 0.0 means all work on one expert
   129→            if avg_utilization > 0:
   130→                sparsity = (
   131→                    1.0 - (max(expert_utilization.values()) - avg_utilization) / avg_utilization
   132→                )
   133→                sparsity = max(0.0, min(1.0, sparsity))  # Clamp to [0,1]
   134→            else:
   135→                sparsity = 0.0
   136→        else:
   137→            sparsity = 0.0
   138→    else:
   139→        sparsity = 0.0
   140→
   141→    # Enhanced decision logic incorporating sparsity and load balancing
   142→
   143→    # Default case: use aiter's tuned configuration
   144→    ks = "default"
   145→    bm = None
   146→
   147→    # Determine if we should use custom KSPLIT based on workload characteristics
   148→    use_custom_ksplit = False
   149→
   150→    # Case 1: Very sparse workloads (few experts getting most work)
   151→    if estimated_m < 25 and sparsity > 0.7:
   152→        # Highly sparse: use KSPLIT=4 for better load balancing
   153→        ks = "4"
   154→        bm = "64"
   155→        use_custom_ksplit = True
   156→
   157→    # Case 2: Moderately sparse but with some expert hotspots
   158→    elif estimated_m < 80 and sparsity > 0.4:
   159→        # Moderately sparse: use KSPLIT=2 as safe middle ground
   160→        ks = "2"
   161→        bm = None
   162→        use_custom_ksplit = True
   163→
   164→    # Case 3: Very large expert counts with moderate load per expert
   165→    elif is_large_expert and estimated_m >= 16 and estimated_m < 80:
   166→        # Large expert count but moderate load: default is usually best
   167→        ks = "default"
   168→        bm = None
   169→        use_custom_ksplit = False  # Explicitly use default
   170→
   171→    # Case 4: Dense workloads - always use default
   172→    elif estimated_m >= 80:
   173→        # Dense workloads: aiter's tuned config is usually optimal
   174→        ks = "default"
   175→        bm = None
   176→        use_custom_ksplit = False  # Explicitly use default
   177→
   178→    # Additional refinement: adjust BLOCK_M based on expert utilization patterns
   179→    if use_custom_ksplit and ks == "4" and bm == "64":
   180→        # For KSPLIT=4 cases, we might benefit from different BLOCK_M values
   181→        # based on the actual workload
   182→        if estimated_m < 10:
   183→            # Extremely sparse: smaller blocks might help with load balancing
   184→            bm = "32"
   185→        elif estimated_m > 50:
   186→            # Relatively dense for sparse case: larger blocks might be better
   187→            bm = "128"
   188→        # else: keep bm="64" as default
   189→
   190→    return ks, bm

   214→
   215→    # Enhanced KSPLIT and BLOCK_M selection
   216→    ks, bm = _select_ksplit_and_block_m(topk_ids, num_experts, is_large_expert)
   217→
   218→    # Update environment variables only when necessary

   220→
   221→    return fused_moe(
   222→        hidden_states,

     1→"""
     2→MLA decode: three-regime routing with adaptive KV splits and metadata caching.
     3→Improved cache efficiency with reduced metadata computation overhead.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import torch
     7→from task import input_t, output_t
     8→from aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd
     9→from aiter import dtypes as aiter_dtypes
    10→from aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1, get_mla_metadata_v1
    11→
    12→SM_SCALE = 1.0 / (576**0.5)
    13→V_HEAD_DIM = 512
    14→NUM_KV_HEADS = 1
    15→QK_HEAD_DIM = 576
    16→PAGE_SIZE = 1
    17→FP8_DTYPE = aiter_dtypes.fp8
    18→BF16_DTYPE = torch.bfloat16
    19→
    20→# ─── Regime thresholds (calibrated Phase 13 v3 optimal) ─────────────────
    21→MATMUL_MAX_BS = 4
    22→MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV = 32768
    23→A16W8_THRESHOLD = 262144
    24→
    25→# Cache for metadata computation results
    26→_cache: dict = {}
    27→# Pre-allocated output buffers keyed on (total_q, nheads)
    28→_out_buf: dict = {}
    29→# Cache for num_kv_splits computations to avoid repeated calculations
    30→_splits_cache: dict = {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv: int) -> int:
    34→    """Adaptive split-K factor based on KV sequence length with caching."""
    35→    if total_kv not in _splits_cache:
    36→        if total_kv <= 2048:
    37→            _splits_cache[total_kv] = 1
    38→        elif total_kv <= 16384:
    39→            _splits_cache[total_kv] = 4
    40→        elif total_kv <= 131072:
    41→            _splits_cache[total_kv] = 8
    42→        elif total_kv <= 524288:
    43→            _splits_cache[total_kv] = 16
    44→        else:
    45→            _splits_cache[total_kv] = 32
    46→    return _splits_cache[total_kv]
    47→
    48→
    49→def _quantize_fp8(tensor):
    50→    finfo = torch.finfo(FP8_DTYPE)
    51→    amax = tensor.abs().amax().clamp(min=1e-12)
    52→    scale = amax / finfo.max
    53→    return (
    54→        (tensor / scale).clamp(finfo.min, finfo.max).to(FP8_DTYPE),
    55→        scale.float().reshape(1),
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def _build_cache(
    60→    bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen, nheads, q_dtype, kv_dtype, num_kv_splits, qo_indptr, kv_indptr
    61→):
    62→    """
    63→    Build metadata cache with optimizations.
    64→    Precompute values that don't change across calls to reduce computation.
    65→    """
    66→    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen
    67→
    68→    # Precompute kv_indices and kv_last_page_len outside the metadata function
    69→    # since they only depend on kv_indptr which changes less frequently
    70→    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
    71→    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
    72→
    73→    # Compute metadata info once
    74→    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(
    75→        bs,
    76→        qseqlen,
    77→        nheads,
    78→        q_dtype,
    79→        kv_dtype,
    80→        is_sparse=False,
    81→        fast_mode=False,
    82→        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits,
    83→        intra_batch_mode=True,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    # Allocate work buffers
    87→    wm, wi, wis, ri, rfm, rpm = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device="cuda") for s, t in info]
    88→
    89→    # Populate metadata buffers
    90→    get_mla_metadata_v1(
    91→        qo_indptr,
    92→        kv_indptr,
    93→        kv_last_page_len,
    94→        nheads // NUM_KV_HEADS,
    95→        NUM_KV_HEADS,
    96→        True,
    97→        wm,
    98→        wis,
    99→        wi,
   100→        ri,
   101→        rfm,
   102→        rpm,
   103→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE,
   104→        kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),
   105→        max_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,
   106→        uni_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,
   107→        fast_mode=False,
   108→        max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,
   109→        intra_batch_mode=True,
   110→        dtype_q=q_dtype,
   111→        dtype_kv=kv_dtype,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    return {
   115→        "kv_indices": kv_indices,
   116→        "kv_last_page_len": kv_last_page_len,
   117→        "work_meta_data": wm,
   118→        "work_indptr": wi,
   119→        "work_info_set": wis,
   120→        "reduce_indptr": ri,
   121→        "reduce_final_map": rfm,
   122→        "reduce_partial_map": rpm,
   123→        "num_kv_splits": num_kv_splits,
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def _matmul_decode(q, kv_data, bs, kvseqlen, nheads):
   128→    """Regime 1: 3D batched matmul — bypasses aiter pipeline entirely."""
   129→    kv = kv_data["bf16"].view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)
   130→
   131→    # True 3D batched GEMM: no broadcast, no string parsing
   132→    q_3d = q.view(bs, nheads, QK_HEAD_DIM)  # [bs, nheads, 576]
   133→    kv_t = kv.transpose(1, 2)  # [bs, 576, kvseqlen]
   134→    scores = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_t).mul_(SM_SCALE)  # [bs, nheads, kvseqlen]
   135→    weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
   136→
   137→    v = kv[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]  # [bs, kvseqlen, 512]
   138→    out = torch.matmul(weights, v)  # [bs, nheads, 512]
   139→
   140→    # Reshape to match expected output: [total_q, nheads, V_HEAD_DIM]
   141→    return out.unsqueeze(1).reshape(-1, nheads, V_HEAD_DIM)
   142→
   143→
   144→def _aiter_decode(
   145→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config, bs, kvseqlen, qseqlen, nheads, total_kv, use_a16w8
   146→):
   147→    """Regime 2/3: aiter mla_decode_fwd with metadata caching."""
   148→    kv_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
   149→    kv_4d = kv_fp8.view(kv_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, kv_fp8.shape[-1])
   150→
   151→    if use_a16w8:
   152→        q_input = q
   153→        q_scale = None
   154→        q_dtype = BF16_DTYPE
   155→    else:
   156→        q_input, q_scale = _quantize_fp8(q)
   157→        q_dtype = FP8_DTYPE
   158→
   159→    num_kv_splits = _choose_num_kv_splits(total_kv)
   160→    key = (bs, qseqlen, kvseqlen, nheads, use_a16w8, num_kv_splits)
   161→
   162→    # Optimize cache lookup by prefetching commonly accessed attributes
   163→    if key not in _cache:
   164→        _cache[key] = _build_cache(
   165→            bs,
   166→            qseqlen,
   167→            kvseqlen,
   168→            nheads,
   169→            q_dtype,
   170→            FP8_DTYPE,
   171→            num_kv_splits,
   172→            qo_indptr,
   173→            kv_indptr,
   174→        )
   175→    c = _cache[key]
   176→
   177→    out_key = (q.shape[0], nheads)
   178→    if out_key not in _out_buf:
   179→        _out_buf[out_key] = torch.empty(
   180→            (q.shape[0], nheads, V_HEAD_DIM),
   181→            dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   182→            device="cuda",
   183→        )
   184→    o = _out_buf[out_key]
   185→
   186→    mla_decode_fwd(
   187→        q_input.view(-1, nheads, QK_HEAD_DIM),
   188→        kv_4d,
   189→        o,
   190→        qo_indptr,
   191→        kv_indptr,
   192→        c["kv_indices"],
   193→        c["kv_last_page_len"],
   194→        qseqlen,
   195→        page_size=PAGE_SIZE,
   196→        nhead_kv=NUM_KV_HEADS,
   197→        sm_scale=SM_SCALE,
   198→        logit_cap=0.0,
   199→        num_kv_splits=c["num_kv_splits"],
   200→        q_scale=q_scale,
   201→        kv_scale=kv_scale,
   202→        intra_batch_mode=True,
   203→        work_meta_data=c["work_meta_data"],
   204→        work_indptr=c["work_indptr"],
   205→        work_info_set=c["work_info_set"],
   206→        reduce_indptr=c["reduce_indptr"],
   207→        reduce_final_map=c["reduce_final_map"],
   208→        reduce_partial_map=c["reduce_partial_map"],
   209→    )
   210→    return o
   211→
   212→
   213→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   214→    """
   215→    MLA decode kernel with improved cache efficiency.
   216→    """
   217→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
   218→    bs = config["batch_size"]
   219→    kvseqlen = config["kv_seq_len"]
   220→    qseqlen = config["q_seq_len"]
   221→    nheads = config["num_heads"]
   222→    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen
   223→
   224→    # Regime 1: 3D matmul for small batches OR small KV (bypass aiter overhead)
   225→    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:
   226→        return _matmul_decode(q, kv_data, bs, kvseqlen, nheads)
   227→
   228→    # Regime 2: a16w8 for medium (skip Q quantization)
   229→    # Regime 3: a8w8 for large (fp8 Q halves bandwidth)
   230→    use_a16w8 = total_kv <= A16W8_THRESHOLD
   231→    return _aiter_decode(
   232→        q,
   233→        kv_data,
   234→        qo_indptr,
   235→        kv_indptr,
   236→        config,
   237→        bs,
   238→        kvseqlen,
   239→        qseqlen,
   240→        nheads,
   241→        total_kv,
   242→        use_a16w8,
   243→    )
   244→
```

     1→"""
     2→MXFP4 GEMM: Fused quant+shuffle + static buffer pre-allocation.
     3→Improved configuration selection with adaptive tile sizing and enhanced caching.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import torch
     7→import os
     8→import ctypes
     9→from task import input_t, output_t
    10→from aiter import dtypes
    11→import aiter
    12→from aiter.ops.triton.quant import dynamic_mxfp4_quant
    13→from aiter.utility.fp4_utils import e8m0_shuffle
    14→from aiter.ops.gemm_op_a4w4 import get_GEMM_config
    15→
    16→_config_cache: dict = {}
    17→_hip_lib = None
    18→_hip_done = False
    19→
    20→# Static pre-allocated buffers — initialized once, reused forever
    21→# Key: (M, K) → A_q buffer [M, K//2] uint8
    22→_A_q_buf: dict = {}
    23→# Key: (M, K) → scale buffer [sm*sn] uint8 (pre-zeroed once)
    24→_scale_buf: dict = {}
    25→# Key: (M, N) → output buffer [M, N] bfloat16
    26→_out_buf: dict = {}
    27→
    28→HIP_SRC = (
    29→    r"""#include <hip/hip_runtime.h>
    30→#include <hip/hip_bf16.h>
    31→
    32→#define BLOCK 256
    33→#define GROUP_SIZE 32
    34→
    35→__device__ __forceinline__ int shuffle_index(int row, int col, int sm, int sn) {
    36→    int d0 = row >> 5;
    37→    int r32 = row & 31;
    38→    int d1 = r32 >> 4;
    39→    int d2 = r32 & 15;
    40→    int d3 = col >> 3;
    41→    int c8 = col & 7;
    42→    int d4 = c8 >> 2;
    43→    int d5 = c8 & 3;
    44→    int stride_d0 = (sn >> 3) * 256;
    45→    return d0 * stride_d0 + d3 * 256 + d5 * 64 + d2 * 4 + d4 * 2 + d1;
    46→}
    47→
    48→__global__ void mxfp4_quant_fused_kernel(
    49→    const __hip_bfloat16* __restrict__ A,
    50→    unsigned char* __restrict__ A_q,
    51→    unsigned char* __restrict__ A_scale_shuffled,
    52→    int M, int K, int sm, int sn)
    53→{
    54→    const int LANES = 16;
    55→    const int num_groups_per_row = K / GROUP_SIZE;
    56→    const int total_groups = M * num_groups_per_row;
    57→
    58→    int global_tid = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;
    59→    int group_idx = global_tid / LANES;
    60→    int lane = global_tid % LANES;
    61→
    62→    if (group_idx >= total_groups) return;
    63→
    64→    int row = group_idx / num_groups_per_row;
    65→    int grp = group_idx % num_groups_per_row;
    66→
    67→    int base = row * K + grp * GROUP_SIZE;
    68→    float v0 = __bfloat162float(A[base + lane * 2]);
    69→    float v1 = __bfloat162float(A[base + lane * 2 + 1]);
    70→
    71→    __shared__ float red[BLOCK];
    72→    int local_group = threadIdx.x / LANES;
    73→    float local_max = fmaxf(fabsf(v0), fabsf(v1));
    74→    red[threadIdx.x] = local_max;
    75→    __syncthreads();
    76→
    77→    int group_base = local_group * LANES;
    78→    for (int stride = LANES / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
    79→        if (lane < stride)
    80→            red[group_base + lane] = fmaxf(red[group_base + lane],
    81→                                             red[group_base + lane + stride]);
    82→        __syncthreads();
    83→    }
    84→    float group_max = red[group_base];
    85→    __syncthreads();
    86→
    87→    unsigned int u32 = __float_as_uint(group_max);
    88→    unsigned int rounded = (u32 + 0x200000u) & 0xFF800000u;
    89→    int exp_biased = (int)((rounded >> 23) & 0xFFu);
    90→    int sb = exp_biased - 2;
    91→    if (sb < 0) sb = 0;
    92→    if (sb > 254) sb = 254;
    93→    unsigned char scale_byte = (unsigned char)sb;
    94→
    95→    float quant_scale = exp2f((float)(129 - exp_biased));
    96→    float n0 = v0 * quant_scale;
    97→    float n1 = v1 * quant_scale;
    98→
    99→    auto encode_fp4_ieee = [](float x) -> unsigned char {
   100→        unsigned int qx = __float_as_uint(x);
   101→        unsigned int sign = qx & 0x80000000u;
   102→        qx ^= sign;
   103→        float qx_pos = __uint_as_float(qx);
   104→        unsigned char e2m1;
   105→        if (qx_pos >= 6.0f) {
   106→            e2m1 = 0x7u;
   107→        } else if (qx_pos < 1.0f) {
   108→            float denormal_x = qx_pos + __uint_as_float(0x4A800000u);
   109→            unsigned int du = __float_as_uint(denormal_x) - 0x4A800000u;
   110→            e2m1 = (unsigned char)du;
   111→        } else {
   112→            unsigned int mant_odd = (qx >> 22) & 1u;
   113→            qx += 0xC11FFFFFu;
   114→            qx += mant_odd;
   115→            qx >>= 22;
   116→            e2m1 = (unsigned char)qx;
   117→        }
   118→        e2m1 |= (unsigned char)(sign >> 28);
   119→        return e2m1;
   120→    };
   121→
   122→    unsigned char fp4_0 = encode_fp4_ieee(n0);
   123→    unsigned char fp4_1 = encode_fp4_ieee(n1);
   124→    A_q[row * (K / 2) + grp * (GROUP_SIZE / 2) + lane] = (fp4_1 << 4) | (fp4_0 & 0x0F);
   125→}
   126→
   127→if (lane == 0) {
   128→    A_scale_shuffled[shuffle_index(row, grp, sm, sn)] = scale_byte;
   129→}
   130→
   131→extern "C" int launch_mxfp4_quant_fused(
   132→    void* A, void* A_q, void* A_scale_shuffled,
   133→    int M, int K, int sm, int sn)
   134→{
   135→    int num_groups = M * (K / GROUP_SIZE);
   136→    int blocks = (num_groups * 16 + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;
   137→    """
   138→    + "hip"
   139→    + "Launch"
   140→    + "Kernel"
   141→    + R"""(
   142→        dim3(blocks), dim3(BLOCK), 0, 0,
   143→        (const __hip_bfloat16*)A,
   144→        (unsigned char*)A_q,
   145→        (unsigned char*)A_scale_shuffled,
   146→        M, K, sm, sn);
   147→    return 0;
   148→}
   149→"""
   150→)
   151→
   152→
   153→def _ensure_hip():
   154→    global _hip_lib, _hip_done
   155→    if _hip_done:
   156→        return _hip_lib
   157→    _hip_done = True
   158→    src = "/tmp/_mxfp4_quant_fused_v2.hip"
   159→    so = "/tmp/_mxfp4_quant_fused_v2.so"
   160→    if not os.path.exists(so):
   161→        with open(src, "w") as f:
   162→            f.write(HIP_SRC)
   163→        try:
   164→            import subprocess as sp
   165→
   166→            compiler = os.path.join("/opt/rocm/llvm/bin", "amd" + "clang++")
   167→            sp.run(
   168→                [
   169→                    compiler,
   170→                    "-x",
   171→                    "hip",
   172→                    src,
   173→                    "--offload-arch=gfx950",
   174→                    "--rocm-path=/opt/rocm",
   175→                    "-shared",
   176→                    "-fPIC",
   177→                    "-o",
   178→                    so,
   179→                    "-D__HIP_PLATFORM_AMD__",
   180→                    "-I/opt/rocm/include",
   181→                    "-L/opt/rocm/lib",
   182→                    "-lamdhip64",
   183→                    "-O3",
   184→                    "-ffast-math",
   185→                ],
   186→                check=True,
   187→                capture_output=True,
   188→                timeout=60,
   189→            )
   190→        except Exception:
   191→            return None
   192→    try:
   193→        _hip_lib = ctypes.CDLL(so)
   194→        _hip_lib.launch_mxfp4_quant_fused.restype = ctypes.c_int
   195→        _hip_lib.launch_mxfp4_quant_fused.argtypes = [
   196→            ctypes.c_void_p,
   197→            ctypes.c_void_p,
   198→            ctypes.c_void_p,
   199→            ctypes.c_int,
   200→            ctypes.c_int,
   201→            ctypes.c_int,
   202→            ctypes.c_int,
   203→        ]
   204→    except Exception:
   205→        _hip_lib = None
   206→    return _hip_lib
   207→
   208→
   209→# Module-level init: compile HIP kernel during import (JIT warmup period)
   210→_hip_lib = _ensure_hip()
   211→_hip_done = True
   212→
   213→
   214→def _get_buffers(M, K, N):
   215→    """Return pre-allocated (A_q, scale_flat, out) buffers for this shape."""
   216→    mk_key = (M, K)
   217→    if mk_key not in _A_q_buf:
   218→        _A_q_buf[mk_key] = torch.empty(M, K // 2, dtype=torch.uint8, device="cuda")
   219→    if mk_key not in _scale_buf:
   220→        sm = ((M + 255) // 256) * 256
   221→        sn = ((K // 32 + 7) // 8) * 8
   222→        # Initialize once with zeros — padding positions stay 0 forever.
   223→        # The quant kernel always overwrites all M*(K//32) active positions,
   224→        # so no stale data accumulates across calls with the same (M, K).
   225→        _scale_buf[mk_key] = torch.zeros(sm * sn, dtype=torch.uint8, device="cuda")
   226→    mn_key = (M, N)
   227→    if mn_key not in _out_buf:
   228→        _out_buf[mn_key] = torch.empty(M, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   229→    return _A_q_buf[mk_key], _scale_buf[mk_key], _out_buf[mn_key]
   230→
   231→
   232→def _kernel_name(tile_m, tile_n):
   233→    sym = f"f4gemm_bf16_per1x32Fp4_BpreShuffle_{tile_m}x{tile_n}"
   234→    return f"_ZN5aiter{len(sym)}{sym}E"
   235→
   236→
   237→def _get_config(M, N, K):
   238→    """
   239→    Improved configuration selection with adaptive tile sizing.
   240→    Uses mathematical relationships to determine optimal tile sizes
   241→    rather than relying solely on hardcoded thresholds.
   242→    """
   243→    key = (M, N, K)
   244→    if key not in _config_cache:
   245→        kernel_name = None
   246→        split_k = None
   247→        csv_found = False
   248→        try:
   249→            cfg = get_GEMM_config(M, N, K)
   250→            if isinstance(cfg, dict):
   251→                csv_found = True
   252→                kn = cfg.get("kernelName")
   253→                if kn is not None:
   254→                    kernel_name = str(kn)
   255→                sk = cfg.get("splitK")
   256→                if sk is not None and int(sk) > 0:
   257→                    split_k = int(sk)
   258→            elif cfg is not None:
   259→                csv_found = True
   260→                kernel_name = str(cfg)
   261→        except Exception:
   262→            pass
   263→
   264→        # Fallback: use known-valid tile configs from runner's .co files
   265→        if kernel_name is None:
   266→            if M <= 64:
   267→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(32, 128)
   268→            elif M <= 256:
   269→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(64, 256)
   270→            else:
   271→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(128, 256)
   272→
   273→        # Split-K for memory-bound shapes (small M, large K)
   274→        # Override even CSV splitK=0 when shape is clearly memory-bound
   275→        if split_k is None and M <= 64 and K >= 1024:
   276→            if K >= 4096:
   277→                split_k = 3  # 8-way split
   278→            elif K >= 2048:
   279→                split_k = 2  # 4-way split
   280→            else:
   281→                split_k = 1  # 2-way split
   282→
   283→        _config_cache[key] = (kernel_name, split_k)
   284→    return _config_cache[key]
   285→
   286→
   287→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   288→    A, B, B_q, B_shuffle, B_scale_sh = data
   289→    M, K = A.shape
   290→    N = B.shape[0]
   291→
   292→    kernel_name, log2_ks = _get_config(M, N, K)
   293→
   294→    # HIP fused quant+shuffle: single kernel launch (faster than 2x Triton dispatch)
   295→    if _hip_lib is not None:
   296→        num_groups_k = K // 32
   297→        sm = ((M + 255) // 256) * 256
   298→        sn = ((num_groups_k + 7) // 8) * 8
   299→
   300→        A_q, scale_flat, out = _get_buffers(M, K, N)
   301→        A_cont = A if A.is_contiguous() else A.contiguous()
   302→
   303→        err = _hip_lib.launch_mxfp4_quant_fused(
   304→            ctypes.c_void_p(A_cont.data_ptr()),
   305→            ctypes.c_void_p(A_q.data_ptr()),
   306→            ctypes.c_void_p(scale_flat.data_ptr()),
   307→            ctypes.c_int(M),
   308→            ctypes.c_int(K),
   309→            ctypes.c_int(sm),
   310→            ctypes.c_int(sn),
   311→        )
   312→        if err == 0:
   313→            A_q_fp4x2 = A_q.view(dtypes.fp4x2)
   314→            A_scale_sh = scale_flat.view(sm, sn).view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
   315→            return aiter.gemm_a4w4_asm(
   316→                A_q_fp4x2,
   317→                B_shuffle,
   318→                A_scale_sh,
   319→                B_scale_sh,
   320→                out,
   321→                kernel_name,
   322→                bpreshuffle=True,
   323→                log2_k_split=log2_ks,
   324→            )
   325→
   326→    # For M < 8 or HIP fallback: Triton quant + shuffle (better for tiny M)
   327→    x_fp4, bs_e8m0 = dynamic_mxfp4_quant(A.contiguous())
   328→    A_q_fp4x2 = x_fp4.view(dtypes.fp4x2)
   329→    A_scale_sh = e8m0_shuffle(bs_e8m0).view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
   330→    out = torch.empty(M, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   331→    return aiter.gemm_a4w4_asm(
   332→        A_q_fp4x2,
   333→        B_shuffle,
   334→        A_scale_sh,
   335→        B_scale_sh,
   336→        out,
   337→        kernel_name,
   338→        bpreshuffle=True,
   339→        log2_k_split=log2_ks,
   340→    )
   341→

     1→"""
     2→MoE sprint variant: KSPLIT=6 for very sparse 257E shapes only.
     3→KSPLIT=4 for moderately sparse 257E, KSPLIT=2 for non-257E.
     4→"""
     5→import os
     6→from task import input_t, output_t
     7→from aiter import ActivationType, QuantType
     8→from aiter.fused_moe import fused_moe
     9→
    10→os.environ["AITER_USE_NT"] = "1"
    11→os.environ["AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING"] = "1"
    12→
    13→_state: dict = {"ksplit": None, "block_m": None}
    14→
    15→
    16→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
    17→    (
    18→        hidden_states, gate_up_weight, down_weight,
    19→        gate_up_weight_scale, down_weight_scale,
    20→        gate_up_weight_shuffled, down_weight_shuffled,
    21→        gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled, down_weight_scale_shuffled,
    22→        topk_weights, topk_ids, config,
    23→    ) = data
    24→
    25→    hidden_pad = config["d_hidden_pad"] - config["d_hidden"]
    26→    intermediate_pad = config["d_expert_pad"] - config["d_expert"]
    27→
    28→    num_experts = gate_up_weight_shuffled.shape[0]
    29→    estimated_m = topk_ids.numel() // num_experts
    30→    is_large_expert = num_experts >= 200
    31→
    32→    if estimated_m >= 80:
    33→        ks = "default"
    34→        bm = None
    35→    elif is_large_expert and estimated_m >= 16:
    36→        ks = "default"
    37→        bm = None
    38→    elif is_large_expert and estimated_m < 5:
    39→        # Very sparse 257E: try KSPLIT=6 (64-way split)
    40→        ks = "6"
    41→        bm = "64"
    42→    elif is_large_expert:
    43→        # Moderately sparse 257E: KSPLIT=4 (proven safe)
    44→        ks = "4"
    45→        bm = "64"
    46→    elif estimated_m >= 25:
    47→        ks = "2"
    48→        bm = None
    49→    else:
    50→        ks = "2"
    51→        bm = "64"
    52→
    53→    if _state["ksplit"] != ks or _state["block_m"] != bm:
    54→        if ks == "default":
    55→            os.environ.pop("AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG", None)
    56→            os.environ.pop("AITER_KSPLIT", None)
    57→            os.environ.pop("AITER_BLOCK_M", None)
    58→        else:
    59→            os.environ["AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG"] = "1"
    60→            os.environ["AITER_KSPLIT"] = ks
    61→            if bm:
    62→                os.environ["AITER_BLOCK_M"] = bm
    63→            else:
    64→                os.environ.pop("AITER_BLOCK_M", None)
    65→        _state["ksplit"] = ks
    66→        _state["block_m"] = bm
    67→
    68→    return fused_moe(
    69→        hidden_states, gate_up_weight_shuffled, down_weight_shuffled,
    70→        topk_weights, topk_ids, expert_mask=None,
    71→        activation=ActivationType.Silu, quant_type=QuantType.per_1x32,
    72→        doweight_stage1=False,
    73→        w1_scale=gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,
    74→        w2_scale=down_weight_scale_shuffled,
    75→        a1_scale=None, a2_scale=None,
    76→        hidden_pad=hidden_pad, intermediate_pad=intermediate_pad,
    77→    )
    78→

     1→import torch
     2→from task import input_t, output_t
     3→import aiter
     4→from aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1, get_mla_metadata_v1, dtypes as aiter_dtypes
     5→from aiter.ops.triton.quant import dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8
     6→from aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd
     7→
     8→GRAPH_CACHE = {}
     9→
    10→def get_metadata(batch_size, max_q_len, nq, nkv, q_dtype, kv_dtype, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len, num_kv_splits):
    11→    # This happens once per shape, we can't easily graph it because it has data-dependent shapes
    12→    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(
    13→        batch_size, max_q_len, nq, q_dtype, kv_dtype,
    14→        is_sparse=False, fast_mode=True,
    15→        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, intra_batch_mode=True,
    16→    )
    17→    work = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device="cuda") for s, t in info]
    18→    
    19→    (work_metadata, work_indptr, work_info_set,
    20→     reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map) = work
    21→
    22→    get_mla_metadata_v1(
    23→        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len,
    24→        nq // nkv, nkv, True,
    25→        work_metadata, work_info_set, work_indptr,
    26→        reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map,
    27→        page_size=1, kv_granularity=16,
    28→        max_seqlen_qo=max_q_len, uni_seqlen_qo=max_q_len,
    29→        fast_mode=True, max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,
    30→        intra_batch_mode=True, dtype_q=q_dtype, dtype_kv=kv_dtype,
    31→    )
    32→    return work
    33→
    34→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
    35→    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data
    36→    
    37→    ptrs = (q.data_ptr(), kv_data["fp8"][0].data_ptr())
    38→    if ptrs in GRAPH_CACHE:
    39→        graph, out, q_fp8_g, q_scale_g = GRAPH_CACHE[ptrs]
    40→        # Quantize Q outside the graph or inside? 
    41→        # Inside the graph is faster. The graph contains the quantize call.
    42→        graph.replay()
    43→        return out
    44→
    45→    batch_size = config["batch_size"]
    46→    nq = config["num_heads"]
    47→    nkv = config["num_kv_heads"]
    48→    dv = config["v_head_dim"]
    49→    max_q_len = config["q_seq_len"]
    50→    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
    51→    
    52→    q_fp8 = torch.empty(q.shape, dtype=aiter_dtypes.fp8, device=q.device)
    53→    q_scale = torch.empty(1, dtype=torch.float32, device=q.device)
    54→    
    55→    kv_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data["fp8"]
    56→    kv_buffer_4d = kv_fp8.view(kv_fp8.shape[0], 1, nkv, -1)
    57→    
    58→    o = torch.empty((q.shape[0], nq, dv), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
    59→    total_kv_len = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())
    60→    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv_len, dtype=torch.int32, device="cuda")
    61→
    62→    num_kv_splits = 32
    63→
    64→    work = get_metadata(batch_size, max_q_len, nq, nkv, q_fp8.dtype, kv_fp8.dtype, 
    65→                        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len, num_kv_splits)
    66→    
    67→    (work_metadata, work_indptr, work_info_set,
    68→     reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map, reduce_partial_map) = work
    69→
    70→    # Warmup
    71→    for _ in range(3):
    72→        dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8(q_fp8, q, q_scale)
    73→        mla_decode_fwd(
    74→            q_fp8, kv_buffer_4d, o, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len,
    75→            max_q_len, page_size=1, nhead_kv=nkv, sm_scale=config["sm_scale"],
    76→            num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, work_meta_data=work_metadata,
    77→            work_indptr=work_indptr, work_info_set=work_info_set,
    78→            reduce_indptr=reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map=reduce_final_map,
    79→            reduce_partial_map=reduce_partial_map, q_scale=q_scale, kv_scale=kv_scale,
    80→            intra_batch_mode=True
    81→        )
    82→
    83→    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    84→
    85→    g = torch.cuda.CUDAGraph()
    86→    with torch.cuda.graph(g):
    87→        dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8(q_fp8, q, q_scale)
    88→        mla_decode_fwd(
    89→            q_fp8, kv_buffer_4d, o, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len,
    90→            max_q_len, page_size=1, nhead_kv=nkv, sm_scale=config["sm_scale"],
    91→            num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, work_meta_data=work_metadata,
    92→            work_indptr=work_indptr, work_info_set=work_info_set,
    93→            reduce_indptr=reduce_indptr, reduce_final_map=reduce_final_map,
    94→            reduce_partial_map=reduce_partial_map, q_scale=q_scale, kv_scale=kv_scale,
    95→            intra_batch_mode=True
    96→        )
    97→        
    98→    GRAPH_CACHE[ptrs] = (g, o, q_fp8, q_scale)
    99→    return o
   100→

     1→"""
     2→MoE: Optimized for Rank 10 (Target: ~151µs, 4µs improvement)
     3→
     4→Quick optimizations based on Agent A2/A3 analysis:
     5→1. Shape-specific block_m (32/64/128 per shape)
     6→2. Aggressive KSPLIT for sparse shapes
     7→3. AITER_USE_NT=1 for non-temporal loads
     8→4. AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG for custom configs
     9→
    10→Benchmark shapes:
    11→  S1: 128tok, 8exp, top2,  d=7168, dexp=2048  -> est_m=32
    12→  S2: 128tok, 256exp, top8, d=2048, dexp=1408 -> est_m=4
    13→  S3: 512tok, 8exp, top2,  d=7168, dexp=2048  -> est_m=128
    14→  S4: 512tok, 256exp, top8, d=2048, dexp=1408 -> est_m=16
    15→  S5: 2048tok, 8exp, top2, d=7168, dexp=2048  -> est_m=512
    16→  S6: 2048tok, 256exp, top8, d=2048, dexp=1408 -> est_m=64
    17→"""
    18→
    19→import os
    20→import sys
    21→import torch
    22→from task import input_t, output_t
    23→
    24→# Enable non-temporal loads (reduces L2 cache pollution)
    25→os.environ["AITER_USE_NT"] = "1"
    26→
    27→# Try to import direct-dispatch components
    28→try:
    29→    import aiter
    30→    from aiter import ActivationType, QuantType, dtypes
    31→    from aiter.ops.triton.quant.fused_mxfp4_quant import fused_dynamic_mxfp4_quant_moe_sort
    32→    from aiter.jit.utils.chip_info import get_cu_num
    33→
    34→    assert hasattr(aiter, "moe_sorting_fwd")
    35→    assert hasattr(aiter, "moe_cktile2stages_gemm1")
    36→    assert hasattr(aiter, "moe_cktile2stages_gemm2")
    37→    assert hasattr(aiter, "silu_and_mul")
    38→    _CU_NUM = get_cu_num()
    39→    _USE_DIRECT = True
    40→except Exception as exc:
    41→    _USE_DIRECT = False
    42→
    43→from aiter import ActivationType, QuantType
    44→from aiter.fused_moe import fused_moe
    45→
    46→# Buffer cache
    47→_buf_cache = {}
    48→
    49→
    50→def _select_block_m_optimized(num_tokens, topk, num_experts, inter_dim, d_hidden):
    51→    """Optimized block_m selection with shape-specific tuning."""
    52→    tile_n = 128
    53→    tg_n = (inter_dim + tile_n - 1) // tile_n
    54→
    55→    # Shape-specific tuning based on benchmark analysis
    56→    if num_experts >= 200:
    57→        # 256-expert shapes: smaller block_m for better parallelism
    58→        if num_tokens <= 128:
    59→            candidates = [32, 64]  # S2: est_m=4
    60→        else:
    61→            candidates = [64, 128]  # S4, S6
    62→    else:
    63→        # 8-expert shapes: larger block_m for efficiency
    64→        if num_tokens >= 1024:
    65→            candidates = [128]  # S5: est_m=512
    66→        elif num_tokens >= 512:
    67→            candidates = [64, 128]  # S3: est_m=128
    68→        else:
    69→            candidates = [64]  # S1: est_m=32
    70→
    71→    best = (float("inf"), float("inf"), 64)
    72→    for bm in candidates:
    73→        max_tokens_padded = num_tokens * topk + num_experts * bm - topk
    74→        tg_num = tg_n * ((max_tokens_padded + bm - 1) // bm)
    75→        rounds = (tg_num + _CU_NUM - 1) // _CU_NUM
    76→        empty = _CU_NUM - (tg_num % _CU_NUM) if tg_num % _CU_NUM else 0
    77→        score = (rounds, empty, bm)
    78→        if score < best:
    79→            best = score
    80→    return best[2]
    81→
    82→
    83→def _select_split_k_optimized(estimated_m, num_experts, d_hidden, d_expert):
    84→    """Aggressive split_k for better parallelism on sparse shapes."""
    85→    # More aggressive than before
    86→    if num_experts >= 200:
    87→        # 256 experts: always use split_k
    88→        if estimated_m < 8:
    89→            return 8  # Very sparse
    90→        elif estimated_m < 32:
    91→            return 4
    92→        else:
    93→            return 2
    94→    else:
    95→        # 8 experts
    96→        if estimated_m >= 256:
    97→            return 0  # Dense
    98→        elif estimated_m >= 64:
    99→            return 2
   100→        else:
   101→            return 4
   102→
   103→
   104→def _get_buffers(M, topk, num_experts, model_dim, inter_dim, block_m, device):
   105→    """Pre-allocate buffers."""
   106→    key = (M, topk, num_experts, model_dim, inter_dim, block_m)
   107→    if key in _buf_cache:
   108→        return _buf_cache[key]
   109→
   110→    max_padded = M * topk + num_experts * block_m - topk
   111→    max_m_blocks = (max_padded + block_m - 1) // block_m
   112→
   113→    bufs = {
   114→        "sorted_ids": torch.empty(max_padded, dtype=dtypes.i32, device=device),
   115→        "sorted_weights": torch.empty(max_padded, dtype=dtypes.fp32, device=device),
   116→        "sorted_expert_ids": torch.empty(max_m_blocks, dtype=dtypes.i32, device=device),
   117→        "num_valid_ids": torch.empty(2, dtype=dtypes.i32, device=device),
   118→        "moe_buf": torch.empty((M, model_dim), dtype=dtypes.bf16, device=device),
   119→    }
   120→    _buf_cache[key] = bufs
   121→    return bufs
   122→
   123→
   124→def _direct_dispatch(
   125→    hidden_states, w1, w2, w1_scale, w2_scale, topk_weights, topk_ids, hidden_pad, intermediate_pad
   126→):
   127→    """Execute MoE via direct CK kernel calls."""
   128→    M, topk = topk_ids.shape
   129→    device = topk_ids.device
   130→    num_experts = w1.shape[0]
   131→    model_dim = w2.shape[1]
   132→    inter_dim_packed = w1.shape[1]
   133→
   134→    estimated_m = (M * topk) // num_experts
   135→    d_hidden = model_dim
   136→    d_expert_approx = inter_dim_packed // 2
   137→
   138→    # Optimized parameter selection
   139→    block_m = _select_block_m_optimized(M, topk, num_experts, inter_dim_packed, d_hidden)
   140→    split_k = _select_split_k_optimized(estimated_m, num_experts, d_hidden, d_expert_approx)
   141→
   142→    # Apply settings
   143→    if split_k > 0:
   144→        os.environ["AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG"] = "1"
   145→        os.environ["AITER_KSPLIT"] = str(split_k)
   146→    else:
   147→        os.environ.pop("AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG", None)
   148→        os.environ.pop("AITER_KSPLIT", None)
   149→
   150→    # Padding
   151→    n_pad_stage1 = (intermediate_pad // 64) * 64 * 2
   152→    k_pad_stage1 = (hidden_pad // 128) * 128
   153→    n_pad_stage2 = (hidden_pad // 64) * 64
   154→    k_pad_stage2 = (intermediate_pad // 128) * 128
   155→
   156→    # Stage 0: Token sorting
   157→    bufs = _get_buffers(M, topk, num_experts, model_dim, inter_dim_packed, block_m, device)
   158→
   159→    aiter.moe_sorting_fwd(
   160→        topk_ids,
   161→        topk_weights,
   162→        bufs["sorted_ids"],
   163→        bufs["sorted_weights"],
   164→        bufs["sorted_expert_ids"],
   165→        bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   166→        bufs["moe_buf"],
   167→        num_experts,
   168→        block_m,
   169→        None,
   170→        None,
   171→        0,
   172→    )
   173→
   174→    # Stage 1: Quant + sort
   175→    a1, a1_scale = fused_dynamic_mxfp4_quant_moe_sort(
   176→        hidden_states,
   177→        sorted_ids=bufs["sorted_ids"],
   178→        num_valid_ids=bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   179→        token_num=M,
   180→        topk=1,
   181→        block_size=block_m,
   182→    )
   183→
   184→    w1_scale_view = w1_scale.view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
   185→    w2_scale_view = w2_scale.view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
   186→
   187→    # Stage 2: GEMM1
   188→    if split_k > 1:
   189→        out_dim = model_dim * 8 if w1.dtype == torch.uint32 else model_dim
   190→        out_stage1 = torch.empty((M, topk, out_dim), dtype=dtypes.bf16, device=device)
   191→        tmp_out = torch.zeros((M, topk, inter_dim_packed), dtype=hidden_states.dtype, device=device)
   192→
   193→        aiter.moe_cktile2stages_gemm1(
   194→            a1,
   195→            w1,
   196→            tmp_out,
   197→            bufs["sorted_ids"],
   198→            bufs["sorted_expert_ids"],
   199→            bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   200→            topk,
   201→            n_pad_stage1,
   202→            k_pad_stage1,
   203→            None,
   204→            a1_scale,
   205→            w1_scale_view,
   206→            None,
   207→            ActivationType.Silu,
   208→            block_m,
   209→            split_k,
   210→        )
   211→        aiter.silu_and_mul(out_stage1, tmp_out)
   212→        a2 = out_stage1
   213→        a2_scale = None
   214→    else:
   215→        out_dim = model_dim * 8 if w1.dtype == torch.uint32 else model_dim
   216→        a2 = torch.empty((M, topk, out_dim), dtype=dtypes.bf16, device=device)
   217→
   218→        aiter.moe_cktile2stages_gemm1(
   219→            a1,
   220→            w1,
   221→            a2,
   222→            bufs["sorted_ids"],
   223→            bufs["sorted_expert_ids"],
   224→            bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   225→            topk,
   226→            n_pad_stage1,
   227→            k_pad_stage1,
   228→            None,
   229→            a1_scale,
   230→            w1_scale_view,
   231→            None,
   232→            ActivationType.Silu,
   233→            block_m,
   234→            0,
   235→        )
   236→
   237→        inter_dim = w2.shape[2]
   238→        if w2.dtype == torch.uint32:
   239→            inter_dim = inter_dim * 8
   240→        a2_flat = a2.reshape(-1, inter_dim)
   241→        a2, a2_scale = fused_dynamic_mxfp4_quant_moe_sort(
   242→            a2_flat,
   243→            sorted_ids=bufs["sorted_ids"],
   244→            num_valid_ids=bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   245→            token_num=M,
   246→            topk=topk,
   247→            block_size=block_m,
   248→        )
   249→        a2 = a2.view(M, topk, -1)
   250→
   251→    # Stage 3: GEMM2
   252→    aiter.moe_cktile2stages_gemm2(
   253→        a2,
   254→        w2,
   255→        bufs["moe_buf"],
   256→        bufs["sorted_ids"],
   257→        bufs["sorted_expert_ids"],
   258→        bufs["num_valid_ids"],
   259→        topk,
   260→        n_pad_stage2,
   261→        k_pad_stage2,
   262→        bufs["sorted_weights"],
   263→        a2_scale,
   264→        w2_scale_view,
   265→        None,
   266→        ActivationType.Silu,
   267→        block_m,
   268→    )
   269→
   270→    return bufs["moe_buf"]
   271→
   272→
   273→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   274→    (
   275→        hidden_states,
   276→        gate_up_weight,
   277→        down_weight,
   278→        gate_up_weight_scale,
   279→        down_weight_scale,
   280→        gate_up_weight_shuffled,
   281→        down_weight_shuffled,
   282→        gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,
   283→        down_weight_scale_shuffled,
   284→        topk_weights,
   285→        topk_ids,
   286→        config,
   287→    ) = data
   288→
   289→    hidden_pad = config["d_hidden_pad"] - config["d_hidden"]
   290→    intermediate_pad = config["d_expert_pad"] - config["d_expert"]
   291→
   292→    if _USE_DIRECT:
   293→        try:
   294→            return _direct_dispatch(
   295→                hidden_states,
   296→                gate_up_weight_shuffled,
   297→                down_weight_shuffled,
   298→                gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,
   299→                down_weight_scale_shuffled,
   300→                topk_weights,
   301→                topk_ids,
   302→                hidden_pad,
   303→                intermediate_pad,
   304→            )
   305→        except Exception as exc:
   306→            if not hasattr(custom_kernel, "_logged"):
   307→                print(f"[optimized] Direct failed: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   308→                custom_kernel._logged = True
   309→
   310→    # Fallback
   311→    return fused_moe(
   312→        hidden_states,
   313→        gate_up_weight_shuffled,
   314→        down_weight_shuffled,
   315→        topk_weights,
   316→        topk_ids,
   317→        expert_mask=None,
   318→        activation=ActivationType.Silu,
   319→        quant_type=QuantType.per_1x32,
   320→        doweight_stage1=False,
   321→        w1_scale=gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,
   322→        w2_scale=down_weight_scale_shuffled,
   323→        a1_scale=None,
   324→        a2_scale=None,
   325→        hidden_pad=hidden_pad,
   326→        intermediate_pad=intermediate_pad,
   327→    )
   328→

I now have a comprehensive understanding of all three kernels and the reference implementations. Let me set up task tracking and begin execution.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**MoE Regression Analysis**: The current `submission.py` has two critical problems:
1. `_compute_routing_metrics()` runs `torch.bincount` + a Python for-loop over ALL 257 experts on every call — this is O(N) GPU→CPU sync overhead per invocation
2. Missing module-level `AITER_USE_NT=1` and `AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING=1` env vars that enable non-temporal loads and optimized sorting
3. Uses KSPLIT=4 in some paths, which per the plan's safety constraint causes overflow on both expert counts

**The sprint reference** uses a simple `estimated_m = topk_ids.numel() // num_experts` — a single integer division, zero GPU sync, zero Python loops.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Why KSPLIT=2 max is a hard safety constraint**: The MoE kernel uses composable_kernel (CK) tiled GEMM under the hood. KSPLIT controls how the K-dimension is split across compute units. With KSPLIT≥3 (meaning 2^3=8-way split), the partial accumulations in fp16/bf16 overflow for large expert dimensions (d_expert=2048), producing ~1e35 garbage. KSPLIT=2 (4-way split) keeps partial sums within bf16 dynamic range.

**Why module-level env vars matter**: `os.environ` calls at module level execute during import (before any `custom_kernel` call). `AITER_USE_NT=1` enables non-temporal memory loads (bypasses L2 cache for streaming data, reducing cache pollution). `AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING=1` enables an optimized token-to-expert sorting kernel.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/kernels/moe-mxfp4/submission.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"\nMXFP4 MoE: Simple four-bucket KSPLIT routing with module-level env vars.\nRestored from sprint/moe_ksplit6_257e.py pattern — no torch.bincount, no sparsity calc.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport torch\nfrom task import input_t, output_t\nfrom aiter import ActivationType, QuantType\nfrom aiter.fused_moe import fused_moe\n\n# Module-level: enable non-temporal loads + optimized sorting before any kernel call\nos.environ[\"AITER_USE_NT\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING\"] = \"1\"\n\n_state: dict = {\"ksplit\": None, \"block_m\": None}\n\n\ndef custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:\n    (\n        hidden_states,\n        gate_up_weight,\n        down_weight,\n        gate_up_weight_scale,\n        down_weight_scale,\n        gate_up_weight_shuffled,\n        down_weight_shuffled,\n        gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        down_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        topk_weights,\n        topk_ids,\n        config,\n    ) = data\n\n    hidden_pad = config[\"d_hidden_pad\"] - config[\"d_hidden\"]\n    intermediate_pad = config[\"d_expert_pad\"] - config[\"d_expert\"]\n\n    num_experts = gate_up_weight_shuffled.shape[0]\n    estimated_m = topk_ids.numel() // num_experts\n\n    # Four-bucket routing — KSPLIT=2 max (HARD: ≥3 causes overflow ~1e35)\n    if estimated_m >= 80:\n        ks = \"default\"\n        bm = None\n    elif estimated_m >= 25:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = None\n    elif estimated_m >= 8:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = \"64\"\n    else:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = \"64\"\n\n    # Update env vars only when bucket changes\n    if _state[\"ksplit\"] != ks or _state[\"block_m\"] != bm:\n        if ks == \"default\":\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\", None)\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_KSPLIT\", None)\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BLOCK_M\", None)\n        else:\n            os.environ[\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\"] = \"1\"\n            os.environ[\"AITER_KSPLIT\"] = ks\n            if bm:\n                os.environ[\"AITER_BLOCK_M\"] = bm\n            else:\n                os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BLOCK_M\", None)\n        _state[\"ksplit\"] = ks\n        _state[\"block_m\"] = bm\n\n    return fused_moe(\n        hidden_states,\n        gate_up_weight_shuffled,\n        down_weight_shuffled,\n        topk_weights,\n        topk_ids,\n        expert_mask=None,\n        activation=ActivationType.Silu,\n        quant_type=QuantType.per_1x32,\n        doweight_stage1=False,\n        w1_scale=gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        w2_scale=down_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        a1_scale=None,\n        a2_scale=None,\n        hidden_pad=hidden_pad,\n        intermediate_pad=intermediate_pad,\n    )\n"
}

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**fast_mode in aiter MLA**: The `fast_mode` parameter in `get_mla_metadata_info_v1` / `get_mla_metadata_v1` controls how metadata buffers are sized and populated. `fast_mode=True` skips some safety checks and uses more aggressive memory layouts — trading correctness margins for speed. Both Gemini and Infinity teams use `True`, while our current production uses `False`. This is a low-risk A/B test because correctness is validated by the runner's `check_implementation()`.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Good — I now have 4 submissions running in parallel:
1. **MoE test** (restored submission.py) — `budb4orp5`
2. **MLA test** (fast_mode=True) — `bq2n09709`
3. **MoE env var probe** — `bo48vaf8l`
4. **GEMM config probe** — `bag5q0jd0`

While those run, let me prepare the GEMM split-K fix (Task 3.2) since I can see the issue in the code. The current `_get_config()` at line 275 only applies split-K when `split_k is None` — but if the CSV returns `splitK=0`, we keep `None` (because the check is `int(sk) > 0`). The problem is that for shape S2 (M=16, N=2112, K=7168), if the CSV returns a config with `splitK=0`, our override at line 275 won't trigger because `split_k` would still be `None` due to the `> 0` check... wait, actually if `splitK=0`, `int(sk) > 0` is False, so `split_k` stays None. Then line 275 `if split_k is None and M <= 64 and K >= 1024` WOULD trigger. So split-K should already be applied.

Let me re-read the GEMM code more carefully to understand the actual issue.

   237→def _get_config(M, N, K):
   238→    """
   239→    Improved configuration selection with adaptive tile sizing.
   240→    Uses mathematical relationships to determine optimal tile sizes
   241→    rather than relying solely on hardcoded thresholds.
   242→    """
   243→    key = (M, N, K)
   244→    if key not in _config_cache:
   245→        kernel_name = None
   246→        split_k = None
   247→        csv_found = False
   248→        try:

   257→                    split_k = int(sk)

   259→                csv_found = True
   260→                kernel_name = str(cfg)

   262→            pass

   266→            if M <= 64:
   267→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(32, 128)

   269→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(64, 256)
   270→            else:
   271→                kernel_name = _kernel_name(128, 256)
   272→
   273→        # Split-K for memory-bound shapes (small M, large K)
   274→        # Override even CSV splitK=0 when shape is clearly memory-bound
   275→        if split_k is None and M <= 64 and K >= 1024:
   276→            if K >= 4096:
   277→                split_k = 3  # 8-way split
   278→            elif K >= 2048:

   280→            else:

   287→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
   288→    A, B, B_q, B_shuffle, B_scale_sh = data

   295→    if _hip_lib is not None:
   296→        num_groups_k = K // 32
   297→        sm = ((M + 255) // 256) * 256

   300→        A_q, scale_flat, out = _get_buffers(M, K, N)

   312→        if err == 0:

   321→                kernel_name,
   322→                bpreshuffle=True,
   323→                log2_k_split=log2_ks,
   324→            )
   325→
   326→    # For M < 8 or HIP fallback: Triton quant + shuffle (better for tiny M)
   327→    x_fp4, bs_e8m0 = dynamic_mxfp4_quant(A.contiguous())
   328→    A_q_fp4x2 = x_fp4.view(dtypes.fp4x2)
   329→    A_scale_sh = e8m0_shuffle(bs_e8m0).view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
   330→    out = torch.empty(M, N, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device="cuda")
   331→    return aiter.gemm_a4w4_asm(
   332→        A_q_fp4x2,
   333→        B_shuffle,
   334→        A_scale_sh,
   335→        B_scale_sh,
   336→        out,
   337→        kernel_name,
   338→        bpreshuffle=True,
   339→        log2_k_split=log2_ks,
   340→    )
```

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**The GEMM split-K issue**: Looking at `_get_config()`, the split-K override on line 275 only fires when `split_k is None`. But if the CSV returns a positive but suboptimal `splitK` (e.g., `splitK=1` meaning 2-way instead of the needed `splitK=3` for 8-way), we'd accept the CSV's value and skip the override. For S2 (M=16, K=7168), we need aggressive split-K to parallelize across CUs — the shape is deeply memory-bound with K being 448x larger than M.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

I'll wait for the probe results before applying the GEMM fix, since I need to know what the CSV actually returns. Let me check on the background submissions.

topk_weights, topk_ids, config,\n    ) = data\n\n    if not _probed:\n        _probed = True\n        import inspect\n        # Probe 1: fused_moe source for env var references\n        try:\n            src = inspect.getsource(fused_moe)\n            ENV_VARS = [\n                \"AITER_BLOCK_N\", \"AITER_BLOCK_K\", \"AITER_WAVES_PER_EU\",\n                \"AITER_NUM_WARPS\", \"AITER_NUM_STAGES\", \"AITER_OCCUPANCY\",\n                \"AITER_USE_PERSISTENT\", \"AITER_STREAMK\", \"AITER_MOE_PADDING\",\n                \"AITER_KSPLIT\", \"AITER_BLOCK_M\", \"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\",\n                \"AITER_USE_NT\", \"AITER_USE_OPUS_MOE_SORTING\",\n            ]\n            print(\"=== fused_moe ENV REFS ===\", file=sys.stderr)\n            for i, line in enumerate(src.split(\"\\n\"), 1):\n                for ev in ENV_VARS:\n                    if ev in line:\n                        print(f\"  L{i}: {line.rstrip()}\", file=sys.stderr)\n                        break\n            print(\"=== END ===\", file=sys.stderr)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"source error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n        # Probe 2: check CK functions\n        try:\n            import aiter\n            for fn_name in [\"moe_sorting_fwd\", \"moe_cktile2stages_gemm1\",\n                            \"moe_cktile2stages_gemm2\", \"silu_and_mul\"]:\n                has = hasattr(aiter, fn_name)\n                print(f\"  aiter.{fn_name}: {'YES' if has else 'NO'}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"CK error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n        # Probe 3: fused_moe module functions\n        try:\n            import aiter.fused_moe as fm_mod\n            print(\"\\n=== MODULE FUNCS WITH ENV REFS ===\", file=sys.stderr)\n            for name in sorted(dir(fm_mod)):\n                obj = getattr(fm_mod, name)\n                if callable(obj):\n                    try:\n                        s = inspect.getsource(obj)\n                        hits = [ev for ev in ENV_VARS if ev in s]\n                        if hits:\n                            print(f\"  {name}: {', '.join(hits)}\", file=sys.stderr)\n                    except (TypeError, OSError):\n                        pass\n            print(\"=== END ===\", file=sys.stderr)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"module error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    hidden_pad = config[\"d_hidden_pad\"] - config[\"d_hidden\"]\n    intermediate_pad = config[\"d_expert_pad\"] - config[\"d_expert\"]\n\n    num_experts = gate_up_weight_shuffled.shape[0]\n    estimated_m = topk_ids.numel() // num_experts\n\n    if estimated_m >= 80:\n        ks = \"default\"\n        bm = None\n    elif estimated_m >= 25:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = None\n    elif estimated_m >= 8:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = \"64\"\n    else:\n        ks = \"2\"\n        bm = \"64\"\n\n    if _state[\"ksplit\"] != ks or _state[\"block_m\"] != bm:\n        if ks == \"default\":\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\", None)\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_KSPLIT\", None)\n            os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BLOCK_M\", None)\n        else:\n            os.environ[\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\"] = \"1\"\n            os.environ[\"AITER_KSPLIT\"] = ks\n            if bm:\n                os.environ[\"AITER_BLOCK_M\"] = bm\n            else:\n                os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BLOCK_M\", None)\n        _state[\"ksplit\"] = ks\n        _state[\"block_m\"] = bm\n\n    return fused_moe(\n        hidden_states, gate_up_weight_shuffled, down_weight_shuffled,\n        topk_weights, topk_ids, expert_mask=None,\n        activation=ActivationType.Silu, quant_type=QuantType.per_1x32,\n        doweight_stage1=False,\n        w1_scale=gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        w2_scale=down_weight_scale_shuffled,\n        a1_scale=None, a2_scale=None,\n        hidden_pad=hidden_pad, intermediate_pad=intermediate_pad,\n    )\n"

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**GEMM Optimization Strategy — Based on Probe Results:**

The probe revealed that shapes S1-S4 have **no CSV config** (return `None`), meaning our fallback is entirely responsible. Key changes needed:

1. **S6 (256,3072,1536)**: CSV says 32x128 is optimal, but our fallback uses 64x256. **Switch to 32x128**.
2. **S1-S4 split-K**: Since CSV returns None, `split_k` stays None, and our override at line 275 fires correctly for shapes meeting `M<=64 and K>=1024`. S2 (16,2112,7168) gets `split_k=3` — this is correct.
3. **The real issue might be tile sizes for small-M shapes**: S1 (M=4) uses 32x128 fallback which is fine, but the actual bottleneck for S2 might not be split-K — it's that **M=16 with K=7168 needs maximum parallelism** and 32x128 tiles might be too small.

Available tiles: 32x128, 64x256, 192x128. For M=256, the CSV prefers 32x128 over our 64x256 fallback — let's fix that.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> TOOL

  "content": "\"\"\"\nMLA decode: CUDA graph capture with fast_mode=True and fixed kv_splits=32.\nBased on Gemini team pattern + our 3-regime routing.\nGraph captures the quantize + mla_decode_fwd pipeline, replays on repeated shapes.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport torch\nfrom task import input_t, output_t\nfrom aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd\nfrom aiter import dtypes as aiter_dtypes\nfrom aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1, get_mla_metadata_v1\nfrom aiter.ops.triton.quant import dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8\n\nSM_SCALE = 1.0 / (576**0.5)\nV_HEAD_DIM = 512\nNUM_KV_HEADS = 1\nQK_HEAD_DIM = 576\nPAGE_SIZE = 1\nFP8_DTYPE = aiter_dtypes.fp8\nBF16_DTYPE = torch.bfloat16\n\n# Regime thresholds\nMATMUL_MAX_BS = 4\nMATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV = 32768\n\n# CUDA graph cache: (compressed_kv_ptr, k_pe_ptr) -> (graph, output, q_fp8, q_scale)\n_graph_cache: dict = {}\n# Metadata cache for non-graphed path\n_meta_cache: dict = {}\n\n\ndef _matmul_decode(q, kv_data, bs, kvseqlen, nheads):\n    \"\"\"Regime 1: 3D batched matmul — bypasses aiter pipeline entirely.\"\"\"\n    kv = kv_data[\"bf16\"].view(bs, kvseqlen, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n    q_3d = q.view(bs, nheads, QK_HEAD_DIM)\n    kv_t = kv.transpose(1, 2)\n    scores = torch.matmul(q_3d, kv_t).mul_(SM_SCALE)\n    weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)\n    v = kv[:, :, :V_HEAD_DIM]\n    out = torch.matmul(weights, v)\n    return out.unsqueeze(1).reshape(-1, nheads, V_HEAD_DIM)\n\n\ndef _get_metadata(bs, qseqlen, nheads, q_dtype, kv_dtype, num_kv_splits, qo_indptr, kv_indptr):\n    \"\"\"Compute metadata buffers (not graphable — data-dependent shapes).\"\"\"\n    total_kv = int(kv_indptr[-1].item())\n    kv_indices = torch.arange(total_kv, dtype=torch.int32, device=\"cuda\")\n    kv_last_page_len = (kv_indptr[1:] - kv_indptr[:-1]).to(torch.int32)\n\n    info = get_mla_metadata_info_v1(\n        bs, qseqlen, nheads, q_dtype, kv_dtype,\n        is_sparse=False, fast_mode=True,\n        num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits, intra_batch_mode=True,\n    )\n    wm, wi, wis, ri, rfm, rpm = [torch.empty(s, dtype=t, device=\"cuda\") for s, t in info]\n\n    get_mla_metadata_v1(\n        qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_last_page_len,\n        nheads // NUM_KV_HEADS, NUM_KV_HEADS, True,\n        wm, wis, wi, ri, rfm, rpm,\n        page_size=PAGE_SIZE, kv_granularity=max(PAGE_SIZE, 16),\n        max_seqlen_qo=qseqlen, uni_seqlen_qo=qseqlen,\n        fast_mode=True, max_split_per_batch=num_kv_splits,\n        intra_batch_mode=True, dtype_q=q_dtype, dtype_kv=kv_dtype,\n    )\n    return kv_indices, kv_last_page_len, wm, wi, wis, ri, rfm, rpm\n\n\ndef _graph_decode(q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, bs, kvseqlen, qseqlen, nheads):\n    \"\"\"Regime 2/3: CUDA graph capture for quantize + mla_decode pipeline.\"\"\"\n    kv_fp8, kv_scale = kv_data[\"fp8\"]\n    kv_4d = kv_fp8.view(kv_fp8.shape[0], PAGE_SIZE, NUM_KV_HEADS, kv_fp8.shape[-1])\n\n    # Cache key: data pointers (same buffers = same shapes, can replay graph)\n    ptrs = (kv_fp8.data_ptr(), q.data_ptr())\n\n    if ptrs in _graph_cache:\n        graph, o, q_fp8, q_scale = _graph_cache[ptrs]\n        graph.replay()\n        return o\n\n    # First call for this shape — build metadata, warmup, capture graph\n    num_kv_splits = 32  # Fixed for graph capture (Gemini pattern)\n    q_fp8 = torch.empty(q.shape, dtype=FP8_DTYPE, device=\"cuda\")\n    q_scale = torch.empty(1, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n    o = torch.empty((q.shape[0], nheads, V_HEAD_DIM), dtype=BF16_DTYPE, device=\"cuda\")\n\n    kv_indices, kv_last_page_len, wm, wi, wis, ri, rfm, rpm = _get_metadata(\n        bs, qseqlen, nheads, FP8_DTYPE, FP8_DTYPE, num_kv_splits, qo_indptr, kv_indptr,\n    )\n\n    # Warmup: 3 iterations to stabilize JIT and memory allocator\n    for _ in range(3):\n        dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8(q_fp8, q, q_scale)\n        mla_decode_fwd(\n            q_fp8.view(-1, nheads, QK_HEAD_DIM), kv_4d, o,\n            qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len,\n            qseqlen, page_size=PAGE_SIZE, nhead_kv=NUM_KV_HEADS,\n            sm_scale=SM_SCALE, logit_cap=0.0,\n            num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits,\n            q_scale=q_scale, kv_scale=kv_scale,\n            intra_batch_mode=True,\n            work_meta_data=wm, work_indptr=wi, work_info_set=wis,\n            reduce_indptr=ri, reduce_final_map=rfm, reduce_partial_map=rpm,\n        )\n\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n\n    # Capture graph\n    g = torch.cuda.CUDAGraph()\n    with torch.cuda.graph(g):\n        dynamic_per_tensor_quant_fp8_i8(q_fp8, q, q_scale)\n        mla_decode_fwd(\n            q_fp8.view(-1, nheads, QK_HEAD_DIM), kv_4d, o,\n            qo_indptr, kv_indptr, kv_indices, kv_last_page_len,\n            qseqlen, page_size=PAGE_SIZE, nhead_kv=NUM_KV_HEADS,\n            sm_scale=SM_SCALE, logit_cap=0.0,\n            num_kv_splits=num_kv_splits,\n            q_scale=q_scale, kv_scale=kv_scale,\n            intra_batch_mode=True,\n            work_meta_data=wm, work_indptr=wi, work_info_set=wis,\n            reduce_indptr=ri, reduce_final_map=rfm, reduce_partial_map=rpm,\n        )\n\n    _graph_cache[ptrs] = (g, o, q_fp8, q_scale)\n    return o\n\n\ndef custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:\n    q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config = data\n    bs = config[\"batch_size\"]\n    kvseqlen = config[\"kv_seq_len\"]\n    qseqlen = config[\"q_seq_len\"]\n    nheads = config[\"num_heads\"]\n    total_kv = bs * kvseqlen\n\n    # Regime 1: matmul for small batches (bypass aiter overhead)\n    if bs <= MATMUL_MAX_BS or total_kv <= MATMUL_MAX_TOTAL_KV:\n        return _matmul_decode(q, kv_data, bs, kvseqlen, nheads)\n\n    # Regime 2/3: CUDA graph capture for larger shapes\n    return _graph_decode(q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, bs, kvseqlen, qseqlen, nheads)\n"
}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**MoE Benchmark: 161.4µs geomean — 9.4µs above the 152µs target!**

Analyzing the routing: our four-bucket scheme applies KSPLIT=2 to shapes where the **aiter default tuned config** would be better. Looking at the sprint reference (`moe_ksplit6_257e.py`), it has a special case: **large-expert shapes (≥200 experts) with est_m ≥ 16 use `default`** rather than KSPLIT=2. Our current code forces KSPLIT=2 on those shapes unnecessarily.

Key insight from stderr: for 257-expert shapes with est_m=17, aiter's tuned config knows the optimal block_m and ksplit. Overriding it with KSPLIT=2 is *slower* than the tuned default.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Let me fix the routing to match the sprint reference's large-expert handling.

> TOOL
